# AI Plan

## Current Product Goal
Build RevCapture into a denied-claims recovery platform that identifies, manages, and resolves denied and underpaid claims.

## Capability Buckets

### Frontend
- Claims dashboard
- Claim detail view
- Review queue UI
- Reporting UI

### Backend
- Claims API
- Claim detail endpoint
- Review workflow endpoints
- Submission pipeline

### Intelligence
- Denial classification
- Correction suggestions
- Recovery scoring

### Workflow
- Review queue system
- Approval and rejection flow
- Audit trail
- Notifications

### Infrastructure
- Worker orchestration
- Job queues
- Logging
- Retry mechanisms

## Current Priorities
- Build claims dashboard UI
- Build claim detail page
- Build review queue interface
- Add dashboard metrics view
- Create claims list and detail APIs
- Add status tracking, logging, retry, and audit support
